# WPT's encoding in yonder, at WPT a699fb16767be39ba3e61a8e8b6bef60383f08ee.

# [excluded] are written by hand; tools/test_yonder_wpt_host.sh encoding --update
# rewrites [passing].

# and the arguments. Each fails with the switch off.
